  let expectedRequests : Nat := 984
  let mut requests : Array (String × String × Bool × String) := #[]
  let payloadLines := if payload.isEmpty then [] else payload.splitOn "\n"
  for line in payloadLines do
    match line.splitOn "\t" with
    | [spelling, home, matcherText, expectedStatus] =>
      unless !spelling.isEmpty && !home.isEmpty && !expectedStatus.isEmpty do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: empty request field"
      let matcher : Bool ← match matcherText with
        | "true" => pure true
        | "false" => pure false
        | _ => throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: malformed matcher field"
      requests := requests.push (spelling, home, matcher, expectedStatus)
    | _ => throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: malformed serialized request row"
  unless requests.size == expectedRequests do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: serialized request count differs: {requests.size} vs {expectedRequests}"
  let readers : Array (String × String) := #[
    ("Complexity.NUPPT.advInput_eq", "TCSlib.Complexity.Randomized.PPT"),
    ("Complexity.NUPPT.inArg_eq", "TCSlib.Complexity.Randomized.Closure"),
    ("TCSlib.Cryptography.uexp_def", "TCSlib.Cryptography.Foundations.Probability")
  ]
  let env ← Lean.getEnv
  let mut catalog : Std.HashMap String (Array Lean.Name) := {}
  for (n, _) in env.constants.map₁.toList do
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | continue
    unless (`TCSlib).isPrefixOf env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]! do continue
    catalog := catalog.insert n.toString (((catalog[n.toString]?).getD #[]).push n)
  let mut verified : Nat := 0
  for (spelling, expectedHome, expectedMatcher, expectedStatus) in requests do
    let ns := (catalog[spelling]?).getD #[]
    unless ns.size == 1 do throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: absent/ambiguous {spelling}; candidates={ns.size}"
    let n := ns[0]!
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: no home for {spelling}"
    let home := env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]!
    unless home.toString == expectedHome do throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: wrong home for {spelling}: {home}"
    let some ci := env.find? n | throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: absent constant {spelling}"
    unless ci.isDefinition do throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: not a definition {spelling}"
    if isInstanceCore env n then throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: unexpected instance {spelling}"
    let matcher := (Lean.Meta.Match.Extension.getMatcherInfo? env n).isSome
    unless matcher == expectedMatcher do throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: matcher metadata differs for {spelling}"
    let status ← Lean.Elab.Command.liftCoreM <| Lean.getReducibilityStatus n
    let statusName : String := match status with
      | .reducible => "reducible"
      | .semireducible => "semireducible"
      | .irreducible => "irreducible"
    unless statusName == expectedStatus do throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: reducibility metadata differs for {spelling}: {statusName}"
    verified := verified + 1
  unless verified == requests.size do throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: catalog count mismatch"
  let mut verifiedReaders : Nat := 0
  for (spelling, expectedHome) in readers do
    let ns := (catalog[spelling]?).getD #[]
    unless ns.size == 1 do throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: absent/ambiguous reader {spelling}"
    let n := ns[0]!
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: no reader home {spelling}"
    let home := env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]!
    unless home.toString == expectedHome do throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: wrong reader home {spelling}"
    match env.find? n with
    | some (.thmInfo _) => pure ()
    | _ => throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: reader is not a theorem {spelling}"
    verifiedReaders := verifiedReaders + 1
  unless verifiedReaders == readers.size do throwError "G06-CONTROL-HARNESS {label}: reader count mismatch"
  Lean.logInfo m!"G06-CONTROL-CATALOG label={label} phase={phase} requested={requests.size} verified={verified} readers={verifiedReaders}"

  let expectedRequests : Nat := 984
  let mut requests : Array (String × String) := #[]
  let payloadLines := if payload.isEmpty then [] else payload.splitOn "\n"
  for line in payloadLines do
    match line.splitOn "\t" with
    | [spelling, home] =>
      unless !spelling.isEmpty && !home.isEmpty do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: empty request field"
      requests := requests.push (spelling, home)
    | _ => throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: malformed serialized request row"
  unless requests.size == expectedRequests do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: serialized request count differs: {requests.size} vs {expectedRequests}"
  let env ← Lean.getEnv
  let mut catalog : Std.HashMap String (Array Lean.Name) := {}
  let mut verified : Nat := 0
  let mut active : Nat := 0
  let mut already : Nat := 0
  for (n, _) in env.constants.map₁.toList do
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | continue
    unless (`TCSlib).isPrefixOf env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]! do continue
    let ns := (catalog[n.toString]?).getD #[]
    catalog := catalog.insert n.toString (ns.push n)
  for (spelling, expectedHome) in requests do
    let ns := (catalog[spelling]?).getD #[]
    unless ns.size == 1 do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: absent/ambiguous imported name {spelling}; candidates={ns.size}"
    let n := ns[0]!
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: no home for {spelling}"
    let home := env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]!
    unless home.toString == expectedHome do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: home differs for {spelling}: {home}"
    let some ci := env.find? n | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: no constant for {spelling}"
    unless ci.isDefinition do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: requested nondefinition {spelling}"
    let before ← Lean.Elab.Command.liftCoreM <| Lean.getReducibilityStatus n
    if before == .irreducible then
      already := already + 1
    else
      let id := Lean.mkIdent n
      Lean.Elab.Command.elabCommand (← `(command| attribute [local irreducible] $id:ident))
      active := active + 1
    let installed ← Lean.Elab.Command.liftCoreM <| Lean.getReducibilityStatus n
    unless installed == .irreducible do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: seal not installed for {spelling}"
    verified := verified + 1
    Lean.logInfo m!"G06-NAME label={label} spelling={spelling} actual={n.toString} home={home} before={repr before} installed={repr installed}"
  unless active + already == requests.size do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: installation count mismatch"
  unless verified == requests.size do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: verification count mismatch"
  Lean.logInfo m!"G06-SEAL label={label} requested={requests.size} active={active} already_irreducible={already} verified={verified}"

run_cmd Lean.logInfo "G06-CONTROL-BODY-BEGIN G06Control_AdvInput_Sealed"

namespace Gate06Sensitivity

theorem advInputBody (n : ℕ) (y : List Bool) :
    Complexity.NUPPT.advInput n y = Turing.pairEncode (List.replicate n true) y := rfl

end Gate06Sensitivity

run_cmd Lean.logInfo "G06-CONTROL-BODY-END G06Control_AdvInput_Sealed"

  let expectedRequests : Nat := 984
  let mut requests : Array (String × String) := #[]
  let payloadLines := if payload.isEmpty then [] else payload.splitOn "\n"
  for line in payloadLines do
    match line.splitOn "\t" with
    | [spelling, home] =>
      unless !spelling.isEmpty && !home.isEmpty do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: empty request field"
      requests := requests.push (spelling, home)
    | _ => throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: malformed serialized request row"
  unless requests.size == expectedRequests do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: serialized request count differs: {requests.size} vs {expectedRequests}"
  let env ← Lean.getEnv
  let mut catalog : Std.HashMap String (Array Lean.Name) := {}
  let mut verified : Nat := 0
  for (n, _) in env.constants.map₁.toList do
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | continue
    unless (`TCSlib).isPrefixOf env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]! do continue
    let ns := (catalog[n.toString]?).getD #[]
    catalog := catalog.insert n.toString (ns.push n)
  for (spelling, expectedHome) in requests do
    let ns := (catalog[spelling]?).getD #[]
    unless ns.size == 1 do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: absent/ambiguous imported name {spelling}; candidates={ns.size}"
    let n := ns[0]!
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: no home for {spelling}"
    let home := env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]!
    unless home.toString == expectedHome do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: home differs for {spelling}: {home}"
    let some ci := env.find? n | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: no constant for {spelling}"
    unless ci.isDefinition do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: requested nondefinition {spelling}"
    let installed ← Lean.Elab.Command.liftCoreM <| Lean.getReducibilityStatus n
    unless installed == .irreducible do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: seal not installed at EOF for {spelling}"
    verified := verified + 1
  unless verified == requests.size do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: verification count mismatch"
  Lean.logInfo m!"G06-SEAL-END label={label} requested={requests.size} verified={verified}"
